Freudental Birth Records, 188x (R. Wiseman)

This file contains birth records from the parish of Freudental, South Russia 
for the decade 188x. This information was compiled by Ralph Wiseman and 
coworkers September 1996/8 from the St. Petersburg Lutheran Evangelical 
archives published by the LDS. Edited by Dale Wahl.

Revised December 2, 2000
